- 2026-02-06 — [[2026-02-06-yt-zPAY2VxfFBk|The Problem with these Smartphone Batteries]] ★★ · tech-industry-commentary, tech-reviews, smartphones _(**why Apple, Samsung, and Google keep skipping silicon-carbon** while Chinese makers ship 10,000 mAh in iPhone-thin bodies; the answer (via industry sources): a **swelling/longevity risk** — silicon expands up to **3×** on charge → cracking → potential **thermal runaway** — so at tens-of-millions scale even a 1-in-250,000 failure rate is **"a dozen exploding Note 7s"**; the carbon absorbs the expansion, some use a steel cage; plus the market reason — the **US is software/ecosystem-locked**, so there's no competitive pressure to take the risk (in other markets a 40%-bigger battery makes people switch); teach-the-mechanism)_

_Marques's dedicated car channel — Marques-fronted, so content is Marques-attributed
(persona/voice data) UNLESS a co-host takes a segment. **Co-host "Miles" attribution has three
shapes here:** (1) `attribution: subject` — Marques solo (the norm); (2) `⚠️mixed` — Marques frames/
narrates but Miles takes a driving segment (only the Marques portion trains the persona); (3)
`⚠️QUARANTINED` (`attribution: co-host`) — a video narrated end-to-end by Miles (e.g. the McLaren
Artura, BMW M5, Lexus LC500h), which trains the persona NOT AT ALL. Always check the row's tag._

## @TheStudio

_Behind-the-scenes / vlogs / how-we-make-videos channel — **ENSEMBLE, hard attribution-
gated.** The ~18-person MKBHD team (Andrew, Mariah, Vin, Michael, Ellis, Rufus, Harper,
Brandon, Tim, David, Eric, Rich, Olivia, Miles, et al.) narrates most of it. **Only
Marques-attributed segments train the persona**; every page carries a `> ⚠️ ATTRIBUTION`
banner and per-line gating. Tags: `attribution: ⚠️mixed` (Marques frames/appears but co-
hosts take segments) or, where no line is confidently Marques, treated as context-only._

- 2021-07-27 — [[2021-07-27-yt-pkuxIy3kFZM|MKBHD Studio Tour 2021!]] ★★ subject · production-filmmaking, biography, creator-business _(**the channel's earliest video** — Marques hosts a first-person tour of the whole studio he built. Production/biography goldmine: the **carpet+ceiling-foam** echo-kill design in every shoot room; the **permanent top-down room** (+ anti-reflection blocker); the **"Mia" camera-robot** ("moves a human literally could never"; "a 6-second robot video takes a lot longer than 6 seconds"); the **phone drawer** (3 yrs of every phone); the **Apple-Park-inspired** 9× Aperture 300D lighting grid; the **battery-station rule** ("if it's up top it's 100% and you can go — the only thing I care about"); **"I'm a clean-surfaces type of guy."** Clean Marques.)_

## @WaveformClips

_Podcast clips channel — each clip is an excerpt of a full @Waveform episode, so the
dedup rule applies: clips whose parent episode is already ingested stay L1 in the ledger
with a `dup-of:<episode-id>` note and get NO page (the full episode is canonical). Only
clips whose parent episode is NOT ingested are L2'd here. Ensemble/multi-host, auto-
captions with no speaker labels → attribution uncertain; only unambiguously-Marques
lines enter the persona/voice banks._
